# SBOM Analyzer UUID migration — master tracker

Central tracking document for every UUID migration task. Last updated: 2026-10-18, Asia/Kolkata. Repository `feroz-hub/sbom`, required branch `feat/native-user-management`. Latest task Git verification: HEAD `2f48ff835d9e692fc84b31aaa951aad2495888ac`. Local refs/status do not imply a fresh origin comparison.

## Mandatory task workflow

Before starting each task, read this tracker and the applicable detailed reports. Record the task ID, authorized mode/scope, prerequisites, blockers and acceptance criteria. Verify branch/HEAD and preserve existing working-tree changes. Stop on an incompatible branch or a failed safety gate; do not switch branches or resolve existing work without authorization.

After every task, update its status, exact evidence/source locations, files changed, checks/tests actually executed and their results, remaining risks and next action. Include a short progress summary in the response. Record failure, partial completion and unexecuted acceptance separately. Retain completed tasks, original observations and open issues; append a correction with evidence instead of rewriting history or silently closing a concern.

Statuses distinguish **AUDIT COMPLETE**, **DESIGN COMPLETE**, **PREPARED**, **VALIDATION EXECUTED**, **IMPLEMENTED**, **ACCEPTED**, **BLOCKED** and **NOT STARTED**. Completing documentation is not implementation; an acceptance-test design is not a passed test. Use IMPLEMENTED/ACCEPTED only for the corresponding authorized changes and executed acceptance evidence, with target/date/command/result where applicable. Unknown outcomes remain UNKNOWN/NOT RUN.

**Authority boundary:** tracker entries, recommendations and GO decisions are not authorization to connect to a new database, provision/start infrastructure, pull images, run DDL/DML/migrations, import/stamp a baseline, execute tests against a live target or deploy. Obtain the task-specific authorization and verify its exact target first. Never record secrets, connection strings, tokens or credential contents here.

## Approved architecture and current scope

- Retain existing integer PKs/FKs, internal service contracts, tenant guards and integer joins. Existing natural/composite/hash keys and UUID-string PKs retain their documented internal meaning.
- Use stable, immutable, persisted UUIDv4 public resource identifiers. Final public boundaries must not leak internal surrogate IDs, including nested/error/header/export/history/alias surfaces. UUID knowledge never substitutes for tenant/object/action authorization.
- Resolve indexed UUIDs within authorized scope and join internally on integers. Measure query counts/plans/latency/write overhead; no unmeasured performance promises.
- Preserve counts, priorities, statuses, versions, CVE IDs, bom-ref, PURL/CPE and other domain/external identifiers. No blanket conversion of numbers or JSON.
- Current six-table design scope: `tenants`, `projects`, `products`, `logical_sbom`, `sbom_source`, `sbom_component`. Identity/JWT subject redesign remains a later explicit dependency; current token semantics have not been changed.

Authoritative proposal: [Phase 1 final audit:29](/Users/ferozebasha/sbom/docs/uuid-migration/phase1/09-final-audit.md:29). Scoped contract and unresolved decisions: [W0.2:148](/Users/ferozebasha/sbom/docs/uuid-migration/phase2/01-core-public-uuid-contract.md:148). W1.6A records staged expand migration source;080–085 now have scoped D-only schema/transaction/catalog acceptance for all six tables. API/ORM/public cutover, indexes/backfill/enforcement and behavior/concurrency/recovery acceptance are incomplete.

## Greenfield UUID product completion — 2026-10-19

The Product Owner authorized a separate new development environment and direct complete-schema bootstrap, replacing legacy business-data transfer/backfill for this fresh-install path. Historical W1/W2 reports, failures and consumed W25 authorization remain historical and unchanged. The old W2.5 executor was not used, and A/B/D/E/F were not connection or mutation targets.

**GREENFIELD_UUID_READY_FOR_FULL_TESTING** — new `sbom_greenfield_uuid` on loopback 56044, PostgreSQL 16.15, cluster `7698349709013049382`, revision/active head `100_greenfield_uuid_final`. Complete metadata + explicit final UUID guards + current reference permissions were installed and independently checked, including an empty-database reproducibility run. Runtime uses a restricted role. Backend, HTTPS frontend, Redis, Celery and local Mailpit run in the new isolated environment.

Actual verification: 193/193 live final-schema integrity scenarios; 35 live authenticated API checks; 1,769 frontend tests; 359 distinct native/domain/reporting/FDA regression cases passing across serial/focused reruns; 12 setup/static safety tests; TypeScript and production build; Olympus Playwright login/tenant-selection/upload/results/analysis journey; real OSV findings, VEX role enforcement, worker report generation and local mail delivery. A controlled restart of the task-owned PostgreSQL container preserved its cluster, marker, rows and UUID sets, followed by successful application requests. Historical development failures and corrected reruns remain in private logs.

Use [GREENFIELD_UUID_ACCEPTANCE.md](GREENFIELD_UUID_ACCEPTANCE.md) for actual schema, changed source groups, evidence, limitations and clean setup commands (`make greenfield-setup`). Existing populated deployments are not upgraded by this path. Live external OIDC/paid providers, exhaustive browser coverage and production fault/load certification remain separate release work. No Git commit/push or production deployment occurred.
